## createHrisAttendance

Create an attendance

### Example Usage

<!-- UsageSnippet language="typescript" operationID="createHrisAttendance" method="post" path="/hris/{connection_id}/attendance" example="hris_attendance" -->
```typescript

const unifiedTo = new UnifiedTo({
  security: {
    jwt: "<YOUR_API_KEY_HERE>",
  },
});

async function run() {
  const result = await unifiedTo.hris.createHrisAttendance({
    hrisAttendance: {
      address: {
        address1: "14108 Allie Flats",
        city: "Kearaborough",
        countryCode: "US",
        postalCode: "23844-2344",
        region: "Tennessee",
        regionCode: "CA",
      },
      approvedAt: new Date("2021-08-13T11:34:46.128Z"),
      breaks: [
        {
          durationMinutes: 12,
          endAt: new Date("2023-11-04T04:18:18.631Z"),
          id: "d60a1001-5a8a-4991-8c21-f4da6036cc87",
          isPaid: true,
          name: "Lunch",
          startAt: new Date("2023-10-28T06:11:52.720Z"),
        },
      ],
      createdAt: new Date("2021-08-10T19:43:18.452Z"),
      currency: "UGX",
      declaredTipsAmount: 161,
      employeeUserId: "<id>",
      endAt: new Date("2024-04-21T06:03:27.974Z"),
      hourlyRate: 53,
      hours: 10,
      id: "48a9f478-dea5-460e-ac02-8056a7ee8806",
      jobName: "Global Creative Supervisor",
      nonCashTipsAmount: 54,
      startAt: new Date("2021-11-10T20:17:01.286Z"),
      status: "CLOSED",
      timezone: "America/Atikokan",
      updatedAt: new Date("2022-01-19T12:54:16.062Z"),
    },
    connectionId: "<id>",
  });

  console.log(result);
}

run();
```
